# TODO: every open change, by family

Reworked 2026-10-19; re-laid out 2026-10-24; regrouped 2026-10-30 into seven categories (Food, Farming, World, General Gameplay, Tech, Large Content, Tooling), each holding feature families named after the feature, as in the player changelog. One `#` heading per category, one `##` heading per feature family with one table, one row per significant feature change, bug fix or rebalance, sorted by status (the order below), then by old family and row number. **Complete** rows are not kept in the tables: they move to the [archive](#archive) at the bottom. Detail stays in the linked writeups ([feature_writeup/README.md](../README.md) is the feature index); a row says enough to pick the work up and points at the rest.

**Ids did not change in the regroup.** Every row keeps its old `family/sub #n` id (the family and subfamily names no longer exist as headings; they survive only as the Id), so writeups, briefs (`briefs/<family>_<sub>.md`), the scratch queue and dated History lines still resolve. Find a row by grepping its id.

Sources folded in: the 2026-10-18 version of this file, every writeup's "At a glance", "Limits and decisions" /
findings, plan and todo sections, the release plans up to 1.0.9, the structure test log
([testing/structures/](../testing/structures/00_plan.md)), `TODO` comments in the code, and the
[scratchpad](scratchpad.md) (emptied into this file twice on 2026-10-19 and again on 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22, 2026-10-23, 2026-10-24 and 2026-10-26; rows from it say
"scratchpad 2026-10-19", "scratchpad 2026-10-21", "scratchpad 2026-10-22", "scratchpad 2026-10-23", "scratchpad 2026-10-24" "scratchpad 2026-10-26" "scratchpad 2026-10-27" or "scratchpad 2026-10-28", "scratchpad 2026-10-29" or "scratchpad 2026-10-30"). Deep scan 2026-10-30: every row checked against the writeups, run logs, changelogs and code; `Tests` column added. Deep scan 2026-10-31: repeated for the work of 10-30 and 10-31 (jar install state, WG30 results, built rows), malformed rows fixed.
The hand-written cooking plan tree (`feature_writeup/cooking.md`) was folded into the `food`, `farming` and a few other
rows on 2026-10-19 and removed; rows citing "cooking plan" came from it, with its tree path where one was given. The
older [todo.txt](todo.txt), [kubejs_todo.txt](kubejs_todo.txt) and [mod_todo.txt](mod_todo.txt) were folded in on
2026-10-18 and are kept as history.

**Implementation briefs (2026-10-21):** every open row (Bug, Planned, Pinned, In Progress) outside Tooling (which has no briefs) has a build plan in
[briefs/](../briefs/README.md), one file per subfamily, linked as `[brief]` in the row's Writeup cell.

**Priorities (scratchpad 2026-10-21):** cornmeal fixes (`dishes/ingredients #14`), can textures (`textures/food #1`),
can crafts (`cans/canning #14`). Cornmeal fixes and can crafts were built 2026-10-21 (Testing); can textures are still open.

**Scratch test queue:** [testing/scratch_queue.md](../testing/scratch_queue.md) holds every live check waiting for a bulk
session: scratch server rounds (`SQ-` ids, prefixed per feature) and a client-window round 2 (`CQ-` ids) that also collects
the "in game" halves left open by earlier runs. Runs so far: [2026-10-22](../testing/scratch_queue_run_2026-10-22b.md) and
[2026-10-28](../testing/scratch_queue_run_2026-10-28.md) (recent feature block, then bot players and the perf suite); results
are also in the queue's Run log. A row's `Tests` cell (see [Test tiers](#test-tiers)) says which tiers are still open.

**History, 1.1.02 hotfix (2026-10-23):** 1.1.02 did not load (Vintage placed features used `tag` for FD's `biome_tag`);
fixed in `backportedVintage-0.1.6-ce-19.2-03` and shipped in 1.1.03 ([vintage_delight.md](../vintage_delight.md) section 2.2).

## How to read a row

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Id | The row's permanent id, `family/sub #n` (e.g. `cans/canning #3`), from the old technical family and subfamily. It no longer matches the headings: rows sit under the feature family they belong to. Ids never change; a new row takes the next free number of the old family/sub it would have had (or of the nearest one) |
| Change | The feature, fix or rebalance, in one line |
| Status | See below |
| Tests | The test tiers the row needs before it is **Complete**, and which have passed: see [Test tiers](#test-tiers) |
| Work | Where the work lands: **patcherMod** (the in-tree Forge mod), **new jar** (a new or other in-tree mod: the ports, More Mooblooms, Incubation Additions), **KubeJS**, **data** (json / datapack / generated), **config**, **tool** (`tools/*.py`), **mods/** (add or remove a jar), **docs** |
| Details | How it is (or would be) built, and what is left |
| Decisions | What has to be decided before or during the work; "none" when it can just be done |
| Writeup | The detailed writeup and section, plus its pending in-game tests |

Status, in table order within a subfamily (Bug first):

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Bug** | A known bug that is not fixed yet |
| **Planned** | Not started, no progress |
| **Pinned** | Planned, held on purpose until something else happens (the row says what) |
| **In Progress** | Started, not complete |
| **Testing** | Implementation complete, at least one tier in its `Tests` cell still open: not built into the pack yet, built but not run, run only on a scratch server or in a GameTest, or live too briefly to count as played |
| **Complete** | Every tier in its `Tests` cell has passed (`+`). Features live since 1.0.4 or earlier that are part of everyday play, with nothing in their writeup saying they were never seen in game, count as `P+` |

### Test tiers

A feature is **Complete** only when every test tier it needs has passed. The `Tests` cell lists the tiers the row
needs, in this order, each marked `+` when passed and `~` when partly passed; a tier the row does not need is left out,
and `none` means nothing is tested in game (docs, decisions, investigations, tools that never touch game data).
Example: `S+ C P T` = scratch passed, client, play and tuning still open.

| Tier | Where | What it proves | Needed by | Passed when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S** scratch | Throwaway dedicated server in the scratchpad, headless harness, GameTest, probe commands, fake players (`SQ-` entries) | The change loads clean and its server-side logic and data are right: recipes, tags, loot tables, worldgen census, commands, `latest.log` free of new errors | Every row that changes game data, scripts or server code | A PASS in a run log or the queue's Run log, or the writeup test ticked as scratch |
| **C** client | A client window: throwaway client or the owner's game (`CQ-` entries) | What only a client shows: textures, models, rendering, tooltips, JEI pages and order, GUIs, sounds, client config, client-server sync | Rows with anything visual or client-side | A recorded client check (CQ- PASS, visual test ticked, owner says it looks right) |
| **P** play | Normal survival play on the real pack (owner's game or the live server), after the change shipped in a deployed version | It behaves as designed in context: real chunks, real mobs, automation, multiplayer, no new errors in the player's `latest.log`, no side effects on neighbouring features | Every row a player can reach | Explicit evidence from play (a test ticked "in game", an owner note). Being deployed, or the owner having played, is not a pass |
| **T** tuning | Owner judgment after play | The numbers (rates, costs, food values, frequencies, durations, balance) are accepted or reset | Rows with tunable values | An owner statement |

Order of work: S before shipping (queue it in the scratch queue in the same session), C in the next client round, P
after a deploy, T last. A failure at any tier sends the row back to **In Progress** (or **Bug** once it shipped).

When a row is done: tick its tests in the writeup, set **Complete**, and at the next sweep move it to the [archive](#archive) (ids never change, so citations keep working; never renumber). New ideas go in the [scratchpad](scratchpad.md) first and
move here when they get a family.

| `baking/baked-goods #37` | Zucchini quiche sequenced assembly fails to load | Bug | S P | KubeJS | Found 2026-10-30 in the owner's `logs/kubejs/server.log` (2026-10-29 22:40): `creative_delight:createsequencedassembly/zucchini_quiche_sequenced_assembly` fails with `Can't modify the empty stack`. The cooked-meat choice (an array of 5 meats in the step list of `global.CD.food(e).assemble`, CD `veggiesdelight/recipes.js` ~line 64) is written as a `create:filling` step with fluid `minecraft:empty` instead of a deploying step. The 1.1.04 changelog says "Zucchini Quiche has a sequenced assembly on a Pie Crust"; it does not exist in game. Cause (scan 2026-10-31): `F.assemble` in `cd_food.js` tests `s.fill` first, and every array has `Array.prototype.fill`, so the array step takes the filling branch. Fix: test `Array.isArray(s)` before `s.fill` (deploying with `Ingredient.of(s)`); the quiche is the only `assemble` caller with an array step (grep 2026-10-31). Not fixed as of 2026-10-31 (`cd_food.js` last edited 2026-10-29 20:07). The fix needs a Creative Delight rebuild and a "Fixed" changelog bullet. | none | [creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md) 30 (`CD.food`), [food_families.md](../food_families.md) `quiche/whole` |

### BBQ skewer overlap evaluation

Opened 2026-10-31 for `dishes/meals #46`. Every BBQ skewer that shares a dish or an ingredient with a pack item. All are ported as upstream and none is in the skewer family yet, so each verdict is provisional. Values are hunger / saturation points.

| BBQ skewer | Overlaps with | Same dish? | Verdict (provisional) | Decide with |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cooked Beef Skewer (8 / 11.2, Strength 50%) | FD Barbecue on a Stick (8 / 14.4) | No: beef, cabbage, grilled vs beef on a stick | Keep both | family fold, `dishes/meals #13` |
| Cooked Lamb Skewer (12 / 19.2, Regeneration 50%) | Veggies Turnip Mutton Skewer (7 / 9.8) | No: mutton only vs mutton and turnip | Keep both; rename to Mutton in lang (D5); value pass (D9) | family fold, D9 |

## Terrain Adaptation

| Id | Change | Status | Tests | Work | Details | Decisions | Writeup |

| `structures/terrain #4` | Avoid straight lines in worldgen noise | Pinned | S C P | patcherMod / data | Pinned 2026-10-30 (user) until the user names where it shows. Find where worldgen noise (biome borders, structure placement) makes straight lines and break them up. Looked at 2026-10-30, nothing changed: the original note (10-23) has no place. Ruled out: the biome tuner (fitness offsets, borders still follow noise contours); structure placement lattices (grouped members are moved onto group grids, separation at most 0.62 of spacing, plus frequency vetoes; the per-mod sets at 0.96 separation no longer place). Candidates that need a look in game: chunk-border cuts of features (trees, lakes, deadwood) at 16-block lines; the DH distant-clip fake seabed (`[worldgen.distant]`, flat at y 48 / 8 with 4 blocks of variation, DH only); cliff edges after the 10-27 cliffs-noise cache (linear between 4-block corners, so straighter edges; postdates the note); straight structure-box edges (ground match, earth/water fill, the beard on `beard_box`) | where it shows (screenshot or coordinates) | scratchpad 2026-10-23, [brief](../briefs/structures_terrain.md), [perf.md](../perf.md) 10.8 |
| `structures/terrain #7` | Underground jungle house spawns under oceans and floods | In Progress | S~ P | patcherMod / data | BUILT 2026-10-30 (patcherMod installed 2026-10-31 00:44). Cause: `subterrestrial:jungle_cabin` (y 6..44) and Terralith's cave structures (y -40..40) use absolute heights; under deep water their box reaches the seabed and the beard carves it open. Now dropped when the box top is within 8 blocks of a water-covered floor (`SeabedClearance`, tag `creativeengineering:seabed_clearance` in `terrain_adaptation.js`: 7 Terralith, 6 Subterrestrial land cabins). Lowering instead of dropping not built. SQ-WG30-D01, D03 (ST-234): D01 PASSED, D03 FAILED 2026-10-30: about 145 starts dropped per world and no seabed hole over any find, but kept cabins and witch huts under the ocean still flood from cave water below an intact seabed (jungle cabin [104, 84], seed 8675309: top y 28, seabed 51, water around its floor) | ocean cabin left out of the tag (it would almost never start); add it? | [structure_tuning.md](../structure_tuning.md) 3.26, scratchpad 2026-10-29 |
| `structures/terrain #1` | Terrain adaptation batch 4 | Testing | S C P | data / patcherMod | BUILT 2026-10-30 (structure_tuning.md 3.30), patcherMod installed 2026-10-31 00:44, not yet booted: soft + trim on the 2 Farmer's Structures ponds (common, carp), the swamp fisherman house and Philip's level-three ruins; carp pond also sunk_start; new tag `template_trim/air` (TemplateTrim#trimAir, air in columns with nothing solid only) for Terralith's spire, because the full trim would drop 110 to 273 snow-only columns per upper layer. So step 2 is built behind `[worldgen.earth_fill] beardFootprint` (default false): those columns count toward the mound limit in the whole-start check. patcherMod installed 2026-10-31 00:44, not yet booted. SQ-WG30-D01, D02 (ST-233) PASSED 2026-10-30 (one island per command, all above sea and ground). Found: the `over water` count label is true when any sample point has a plant on the ground (`WORLD_SURFACE_WG` counts grass, `OCEAN_FLOOR_WG` does not), so 2 land blacksmith islands were logged over water | none | [structure_tuning.md](../structure_tuning.md) 3.26, scratchpad 2026-10-29 |

SWAP keeps its shape and turns its outer and middle layer from the floor up into the column's old floor block (a mound of sea floor around the opening); CUT cuts it flush (blocks above the floor become water, the rim becomes the floor block); opened cavities fill with water; the opening into the crystals stays. Prismarine (untouched) geodes are left alone. Headless OceanGeodesTest 13/13; patcherMod installed 2026-10-31 00:44, not yet booted. Scratch SQ-TER-06 (ST-305). # Large Content

Alcohol, mod ports and additions, Creative Delight, Creative Brewing, karma, Creative Labels.

## Alcohol

Alcohol is the two ports plus the pack around them, grouped here from `largecontent` and the other families on
2026-10-19: `brewinport` and `vineryport` (the two jars) and `pack` (pack-side alcohol rows). The open calls are in the
decisions table under the main one. Rows that stay elsewhere because they cover more than alcohol: `pack/release #5`
(`/dumpall` refresh with the ports), `ui/names #2` (`vinery:trippy` lang), `jei/jei-coverage #2` (Vinery world
interactions). Both port jars (`-02`) are live since 1.0.8. First in-game results 2026-10-20 (local `-03`, [alcohol.md](../alcohol.md) 10.18:
bottle sizes, box looks, Stained Glass, wine box and flying fixes); AL-01 to AL-72 and VB-01 to VB-66 are still the first
job before any balance change.

### Decisions

Open calls, recommendation first, sorted Open, Partly decided, Pinned, Decided, Done. Decided and Done rows stay until
their tests pass. "Built with" means the jar already does the recommendation and the call only confirms it or asks
for a change.

| # | Decision | Status | Options (recommended first) | Blocks | Writeup |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 12 | 1.3.10 reference tests (VN-, VJ-) | Open | A: retire them (the VB- suite covers the port); B: rewrite for the port. VJ- can no longer run: patcherMod's pages were deleted 2026-10-27 | alcohol/vineryport #8 | [vinery.md](../vinery.md), [vinery_jei.md](../vinery_jei.md) |

## Karma

Karma example actions, moved verbatim from the scratchpad:
- Good: planting saplings (low limit); bonemeal to compost (low); trading with villagers (low); helping in raids
  (cooldown, high); killing pillagers and witches (cooldown, low); killing the Ender Dragon (exponential decrease,
  negative offset after x kills?); breeding animals (linear decrease to 0, low); taming pets (linear decrease to low,
  low); playing with pets (high karma, high limit); setting pet spawn points (linear decrease to low, cooldown, high);
  curing zombie villagers (slow linear decrease to low, high); tilling, planting, harvesting, planting flowers (high
  limit, slow linear increase, 5 min cooldown); food pantry server feature?; writing books, signing albums (linear
  decrease to low, high boost, high limit); taking photos (low boost, low limit); Via Romana path creation (linear
  increase to high, high limit).
- Bad: breaking claimed beds (small for villager-owned, large for player-owned, linear increase on repeats); being
  mean (no cooldown, high limit: talking rude to MCA villagers, punching villagers or baby animals, killing villagers,
  more for baby villagers and golems); pet respawning (high limit, exponential, no cooldown); killing pacifist or
  protected mobs (exponential, high limit, 1 min cooldown: bats, dolphins, axolotls, pandas, polar bears, elephants,
  whales, Alex's Mobs with no drops: review); killing animals (slow linear from low, 10 s cooldown, buffer of no
  effect); igniting TNT (constant low, no cooldown); breaking a village bell (exponential, no cooldown, high);
  breaking wild waystones (small linear if never activated, large exponential if another player activated it, and a
  chat announcement naming the destroyer); igniting a Nether portal (failing: one large decrease with a low limit,
  chat announcement the first time per player; succeeding: slow linear decrease, low limit, first-time announcement);
  attacking neutral mobs (slow linear, high cap: zombie pigmen, passive spiders, hitting or looking at endermen);
  stealing honey without a campfire (low exponential, low limit); refuelling a boat (slow linear, low limit); falling
  into the void (exponential, 5 min cooldown, high limit); looting another player's corpse (quick exponential, high
  limit); killing a named mob (one large decrease per mob; owner 2026-10-30; hook in patcherMod `mobs.NamedMobs.onDeath`,
  `mobs/tweaks #6`).

| Id | Change | Status | Tests | Work | Details | Decisions | Writeup |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Example actions in the migrated list below | everything | scratchpad 2026-10-19 (list below) |

## Creative Delight Framework

The Creative Delight jar (`development/creativeDelight`) and everything that moves into it. Opened 2026-10-25 from
[creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md); rows were taken from `ports/creativedelightport` and `food/types-and-tuning #14`.
Subfamilies are A to Z, which is also the work order: **framework** (loader, build, tests, dict, config, harness) first, then
**members** (each Delight mod's rows and Java moving in), **qol** (QoL-1 to QoL-6 leaving patcherMod and the pack folder),
**swappers** (later). A member row needs framework #2 (install), #4 (dict) and #6 (move-diff harness) first; a QoL row needs framework #5 (config key) and, for
Java, #8 (compartments). Owner rule: no ongoing backport or merge features beyond what these rows say.

## Creative Brewing

A new own mod, Creative Brewing, planned to hold what Creative Delight does not take (owner, 2026-10-25: "mark all potion brewing, alcohol, vinery, and effect descriptions, changes, shuffles to be moved to a new mod, Creative Brewing"). Nothing moved yet.

| Id | Change | Status | Tests | Work | Details | Decisions | Writeup |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `creativebrewing/move #1` | Move potion brewing, alcohol, Vinery and effect content into Creative Brewing | Planned | S P | KubeJS / new jar | Marked 2026-10-25 during the CD food move (creative_delight.md 29.3). Left in the pack for it: `startup_scripts/cooking/potions.js`, `startup_scripts/cooking/alcohol_drinks.js`, `server_scripts/cooking/alcohol.js`, the effect swaps and Farmer's Respite cake-slice effects in `startup_scripts/cooking/food_values.js`, the Brewin' and Chewin' food rows in `server_scripts/cooking/cooking.js` (pierogi, pizza, ham and cheese sandwich, quiches, horror lasagna, creamy onion soup, cheesy pasta, vegetable omelet), the Brewin' tags in `server_scripts/tags/cookingtags.js`, the effect text (`other/jeed_providers.js`, JEED and tooltip effect lines), and the Brewin' and Vinery ports. Needs: the mod project, its own script trees like CD's, and a call on whether alcohol-side food rows (Brewin' dishes) go there or to CD | open: the mod's scope edge with CD (Brewin' dishes, ciders) | [creative_delight.md](../creative_delight.md) 29.3 |
